- **comfyui-critic**: Infrastructure helper for the comfyui extension's opt-in auto-refine loop. Given the filesystem path to a rendered PNG, the prompt it was rendered from, optional negative / protocol / background / guidance, an optional explicit acceptance criteria, and a hint of which repair channels are available, it READS the image and judges it domain-neutrally against that request, then returns ONLY one JSON decision object (verdict, score, assessment, classified issues, and a single proposed repair action). Never writes files; never asks questions; never influences the active turn directly. Fresh context… ([weikinhuang/dotfiles](https://github.com/weikinhuang/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/config/pi/agents/comfyui-critic.md) · ★21)
- **comfyui-enhance**: Infrastructure helper for the comfyui extension's prompt enhancer. Given a positive prompt, a baseline negative, the target image model's prompting protocol, optional guidance, and optional background context, it normalizes, translates, and refines them into that model's native protocol (booru tags, natural language, …). Returns ONLY a JSON object {"prompt", "negative"}. Never reads or writes files; never asks questions; never influences the active turn directly. Fresh context every invocation. ([weikinhuang/dotfiles](https://github.com/weikinhuang/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/config/pi/agents/comfyui-enhance.md) · ★21)

- **comm-log**: Every message written to the router, captured at the wire, plus event rows (#139). swp08.Client.OnTx fires once per message from transmit (after ACK, NAK or give-up, with Tries) or for messages still queued when a session ends. sendMsg logs unsent when Send refuses. Each Send carries a swp08.Why. OnTx only pushes onto txq (it never blocks the ACK-gated writer). logSent decodes, appends to SentLog ([sethdotfm/sode](https://github.com/sethdotfm/sode/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/comm-log.md))
- **comm-payload**: 典型 Overhead 估算（以 S-band DVB-S2X 為例）： - MAC Frame Header: ~4 bytes / 1500 bytes = 0.27% - RLC/ARQ: 假設重傳率 5% → 5% 額外開銷 - PDCP Header: ~2 bytes / 1500 bytes = 0.13% - 總 Overhead: ~5.4% ([chiikawa1115/cubesat-team](https://github.com/chiikawa1115/cubesat-team/blob/HEAD/agents/comm-payload.md) · ★2)

- **commands-without-prompts**: An agent tool asks the owner before it runs a command it judges risky. Each such prompt stops the owner, often for a command that was harmless, and an owner who is asked too often starts to allow everything. Write commands the tool can see are safe, so it never needs to ask. What follows is what such tools look for, and the same habits suit every tool. ([naima-project/naima](https://github.com/naima-project/naima/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/commands-without-prompts.md) · ★3)

- **comment-ablation-auditor**: Adversarial reviewer of one comment-ablation diff. Reads the removed `#` blocks and the surviving ones in an assigned file group, re-derives each deleted fact's recoverability from the code alone, and reports either CLEAN-CUT (lossless) or DEEP-CUT (a fact the code cannot supply, with the keep class it should have survived under) — plus EARNED or UNEARNED for every surviving comment. Read-only: its tool set has no edit capability. Never reads the ablation's own reports, never invokes kata, never fixes anything. One-off, scoped to the ablation campaign's review wave. ([taylorsatula/mira](https://github.com/taylorsatula/mira/blob/HEAD/.pi/agents/comment-ablation-auditor.md) · ★485)
- **comment-ablator**: Comment-ablation executor for one assignment of files. Reads the comment-ablation skill first, then deletes `#` comments the code already supplies, keeping only the four narrow classes, reporting contradictions and work orders instead of fixing them, and proving every file code-inert with the skill's gate. Reads a baseline before editing so a gate failure is attributable to its own change. One-off, scoped to the ablation campaign. Never touches string literals, docstrings, non-comment lines, or AGENTS.md maps; never commits. ([taylorsatula/mira](https://github.com/taylorsatula/mira/blob/HEAD/.pi/agents/comment-ablator.md) · ★485)

- **comment-analyzer**: Use this agent during PR review to check that a PR's comments and doc-comments are TRUE. It verifies every load-bearing claim in a changed comment against the code it describes, and flags comments the diff left stale. It does not ask for more comments - this repo's committed CLAUDE.md rules default to no comment, so requests to add or expand one are Suggestions at most. It also enforces the repo's load-bearing markers (@deprecated removal versions, TODO(vNN) forms). Comments that verify are endorsed so the reviewer knows accuracy was checked. Condenses ALREADY-CORRECT proposed comment text to a published cap, working from a deliberately narrow input -- the block's KIND, the original block, the edited text, the cap and the style sheet -- and never from the reasoning that produced the edit. Refuses to shorten a docstring, refuses a block whose kind is unresolved, and reports a block it cannot condense rather than cutting evidence. - **comment-review-function-context**: One of four parallel reviewers dispatched by the /comment-review skill. - **comment-review-module-context**: One of four parallel reviewers dispatched by the /comment-review skill. Flags two or three announced subjects, banners reading as chapter breaks, a name in the module's public surface the docstring never accounts for, and a name in the docstring that is not in the surface. - **comment-review-ownership-context**: The reviewer every /comment-review run carries, dispatched with the others by the skill. - **comment-review-review**: Stage 8 of the /comment-review skill. - **comment-style**: How comments are written in the repo's shell code. The style is compact: say the non-obvious *why* once, anchor it to an ADR, and stop. This doc is the convention a `docs(installer): Compact comments` pass produced and future edits should hold to, so comments don't re-inflate. ([Aquastias/Arch-Dotfiles](https://github.com/Aquastias/Arch-Dotfiles/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/comment-style.md))
- **comment-style-enforcer**: Conforms the comments in a set of changes to the full house style — why-over-what, ruthless concision, correct placement, keeping each comment next to the code it explains (splitting big block comments and moving the pieces down), dashed-rule section labels, and referencing another file only when the file actually depends on it (no naming a reusable module's consumers). Use proactively after writing or modifying code and before committing, to bring the comments across the uncommitted changes or the current branch's diff up to style. ([JakeNeau/Jake-Neau-NixOS-Config](https://github.com/JakeNeau/Jake-Neau-NixOS-Config/blob/HEAD/modules/programs/claude-code/config/agents/comment-style-enforcer.md))
- **comment-sweeper**: Puts every comment a diff adds or edits through the comment style guide's sweep and repairs the ones that fail, without ever changing code. Use it for "sweep the comments on this branch", "check the comments in this PR", "go over the comments in this diff", as the comment sweep in the develop skill's step 8, and after any work that added or rewrote comments. ([Runt9-Productions/runt9-claude-harness](https://github.com/Runt9-Productions/runt9-claude-harness/blob/HEAD/plugins/develop/agents/comment-sweeper.md) · ★1)
- **comment-sweeper**: Runs comment-cut's Phase 3 mechanical sweep on an explicit file list — detector deletions and ticket-ref strips by position only, no judgement, no rewording. Dispatched by the comment-cut skill. ([shawnroos/shrimpshack](https://github.com/shawnroos/shrimpshack/blob/HEAD/plugins/comment-cut/agents/comment-sweeper.md))
- **comment-system**: You are Voice, the StickerNest Comment System Employee. Your job is to enable meaningful conversation without toxicity. ([hkcm91/StickerNestV4](https://github.com/hkcm91/StickerNestV4/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/comment-system.md))
- **comment-tightener**: Drives every comment block in a project down to a configured line budget (default one line) through the static-x baseline/ratchet loop — rewriting for density, never truncating. Use when asked to shorten comments across a codebase, enforce a comment length limit, or clean up generated comment bloat. Works with any static-x language pack that ships the comments family. ([thegoldenmule/static-x](https://github.com/thegoldenmule/static-x/blob/HEAD/agents/comment-tightener.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **comment-triage**: Collects a PR's review comments, drops the ones already resolved or addressed, and assesses each remaining one against the actual code. Invoke from gh-address-comments before the human triage. ([sbnet/flux](https://github.com/sbnet/flux/blob/HEAD/plugins/flux/agents/comment-triage.md))
- **comment-triager**: address-review の分類の Workflow が最初に起動する分類担当。PR のレビュー指摘の全件を 1 つの context でまとめて読み、PR の head のコードと主ブランチの先端の方針に照らして fix・decline・out_of_scope・needs_decision・unverified のどれかに分け、根拠の path:line と日本語の返信の草案を返す。ファイルを変えない。 ([yoshiysh/claude-plugins](https://github.com/yoshiysh/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/.agents/skills/address-review/agents/comment-triager.md) · ★1)
- **comment-trimmer**: Use once per work set as the comment-trim stage at completion, before the adversarial review round, or when asked to trim a diff's code comments; never mid-implementation, where tidying your own comments is ordinary editing, and never again for a correction batch after review. Dispatch with Worktree and Base or range, plus Commit subject when the work is delivered as commits, and nothing else. Edits code comments only, by applying the trim-comments skill. ([giostriquer/workshop](https://github.com/giostriquer/workshop/blob/HEAD/plugins/workbench/agents/comment-trimmer.md))
- **comment-unit-auditor**: Judges every comment on ONE unit of code -- a member, a type declaration, or a file header -- against the project code comment policy. Decides for each whether it should exist at all, and if it survives, the smallest form that carries what justified it. Read-only, returns a compact fixed-shape verdict list. Dispatched per unit by comment-auditor; rarely invoked directly. ([LynxStar/MistLang](https://github.com/LynxStar/MistLang/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/comment-unit-auditor.md))
